                 x-data="{ 
                    userRating: 0,

                    hasImdb: true,
                    imdbRating: 8.2,
                    hoverRating: 0,
                    isRating: false,
                    isLoggedIn: false,
                    votingAccess: 'all',
                    rate(value) {
                        if (this.votingAccess === 'members' && !this.isLoggedIn) {
                             if(confirm('Oy kullanmak için üye olmalısınız. Giriş yapmak veya Kayıt olmak ister misiniz?')) {
                                window.location.href = 'https://filmizlehell.net/giris';
                            }
                            return;
                        }

                        if (this.userRating > 0 || this.isRating) return;
                        this.isRating = true;
                        
                        fetch('https://filmizlehell.net/film/3730/puan-ver', {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: {
                                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': '9cCFG37dw9I0qxNfFMoh7chWVAw6HXZM0YlQ6Pji',
                                'Accept': 'application/json'
                            },
                            credentials: 'same-origin',
                            body: JSON.stringify({ rating: value })
                        })
                        .then(res => res.json())
                        .then(data => {
                            if(data.user_rating) {
                                this.userRating = data.user_rating;
                                this.siteRating = Number(data.avg);
                                this.siteVoteCount = data.count;
                            } else if (data.status === 'error') {
                                alert(data.message);
                            }
                        })
                        .catch(err => console.error(err))
                        .finally(() => this.isRating = false);
                    }

Genç kahraman, bir yandan lise hayatının getirdiği zorluklarla mücadele ederken, diğer yandan Örümcek Adam kimliğiyle suçla savaşıyor ve Yenilmezler&#039;in bir üyesi olarak küresel tehditlere karşı sorumluluk üstleniyor.                      method: 'POST',
                     headers: {
                         'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                         'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name=csrf-token]').content,
                         'Accept': 'application/json'

                 });
                 
                 const data = await response.json();
                 
                 if (data.success) {
                     this.likes = data.likes_count;
                     this.dislikes = data.dislikes_count;
                     this.userVote = data.user_vote || '';

    <!-- TRAILER MODAL -->
    <template x-if="isTrailerModalOpen">
        <div class="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
            <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-md" @click="isTrailerModalOpen = false"></div>
            <div class="w-full max-w-4xl aspect-video relative z-[101] shadow-2xl border border-white/10 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-black">
                <button @click="isTrailerModalOpen = false" class="absolute top-4 right-4 z-10 bg-black/50 text-white rounded-full p-2 hover:bg-accent transition-colors">
                    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M18 6 6 18"/><path d="m6 6 12 12"/></svg>
                </button>
                                    <iframe src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/ulA9SpaU-Gg?autoplay=1" class="w-full h-full" frameborder="0" allow="autoplay; encrypted-media" allowfullscreen></iframe>
                            </div>
        </div>

    <!-- END Main Grid -->
    <script>
        window.turnstileLoaded = false;
        window.loadTurnstile = function() {
            if (window.turnstileLoaded) return;
            var script = document.createElement('script');
                                        script.src = 'https://www.google.com/recaptcha/api.js';
                        script.async = true;
            script.defer = true;
            document.body.appendChild(script);
            window.turnstileLoaded = true;
        };

        window.setupReportModal = function() {
            return {
                reportType: '',
                reportDescription: '',
                reportLoading: false,
                reportError: null,
                reportSuccess: null,
                wantTelegram: false,
                telegramUsername: '',
                telegramBotUrl: null,
                telegramLinked: false,
                init: function() {
                },
                submitReport: function() {
                    if (!this.reportType) {
                        this.reportError = 'Lütfen bir hata türü seçin.';
                        return;
                    }
                    var form = this.$refs.reportForm;
                    var tokenInput = form.querySelector('[name="cf-turnstile-response"]') || form.querySelector('[name="g-recaptcha-response"]');
                    if (!tokenInput || !tokenInput.value) {
                        this.reportError = 'Lütfen güvenliği doğrulayın.';
                        return;
                    }

                    var rootEl = this.$root; 
                    var url = rootEl.dataset.url;
                    var csrfToken = rootEl.dataset.csrf;

                    this.reportLoading = true;
                    this.reportError = null;
                    var xsrfToken = '';
                    var parts = ("; " + document.cookie).split("; XSRF-TOKEN=");
                    if (parts.length === 2) {
                        xsrfToken = decodeURIComponent(parts.pop().split(";").shift());
                    }
                    var self = this;
                    fetch(atob('aHR0cHM6Ly9maWxtaXpsZWhlbGwubmV0L2FqYXgvcmVwb3J0LXN0b3Jl'), {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: {
                            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken,
                            'X-XSRF-TOKEN': xsrfToken
                        },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            movie_id: '3730',
                            type: this.reportType,
                            description: this.reportDescription,
                            telegram_username: this.wantTelegram ? this.telegramUsername : null,
                            'cf-turnstile-response': tokenInput.value,
                            'g-recaptcha-response': tokenInput.value
                        })
                    }).then(function(res) {
                        return res.json();
                    }).then(function(data) {
                        self.reportLoading = false;
                        if (data.status === 'success') {
                            self.reportSuccess = data.message;
                            self.telegramBotUrl = data.telegram_bot_url;
                            self.telegramLinked = data.telegram_linked;

                            // If user chose Telegram and bot URL is available, keep modal open for them to click
                            if (self.wantTelegram && data.telegram_bot_url && !data.telegram_linked) {
                                // Keep open so they can click the Telegram button
                            } else {
                                setTimeout(function() {
                                    self.$dispatch('close-report-modal');
                                    self.resetState();
                                }, 2500);
                            }
                        } else {
                            self.reportError = data.message;
                            if (window.turnstile) { try { window.turnstile.reset('#report-turnstile'); } catch(e) {} }
                            if (window.grecaptcha) { try { window.grecaptcha.reset(); } catch(e) {} }
                        }
                    }).catch(function() {
                        self.reportLoading = false;
                        self.reportError = 'Bir hata oluştu.';
                    });
                },
                resetState: function() {
                    this.reportSuccess = null;
                    this.reportError = null;
                    this.reportType = '';
                    this.reportDescription = '';
                    this.telegramBotUrl = null;
                    this.wantTelegram = false;
                }
            };
        };

        window.setupCommentModal = function() {
            return {
                comment: '',
                name: '',
                email: '',
                hasSpoiler: false,
                loading: false,
                error: null,
                success: null,
                quotedReply: null,
                submitComment: function() {
                    if (this.comment.length < 3) {
                        this.error = 'Yorum en az 3 karakter olmalıdır.';
                        return;
                    }
                    var form = this.$refs.commentForm;
                    var tokenInput = form.querySelector('[name="cf-turnstile-response"]') || form.querySelector('[name="g-recaptcha-response"]');
                    if (!tokenInput || !tokenInput.value) {
                        this.error = 'Lütfen güvenliği doğrulayın.';
                        return;
                    }
                    var parentId = this.quotedReply ? this.quotedReply.id : null;
                    var csrfMeta = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]');
                    var csrfToken = this.$el.dataset.csrf || (csrfMeta ? csrfMeta.getAttribute('content') : '');
                    var xsrfToken = '';
                    var parts = ('; ' + document.cookie).split('; XSRF-TOKEN=');
                    if (parts.length === 2) {
                        xsrfToken = decodeURIComponent(parts.pop().split(';').shift());
                    }
                    var self = this;
                    this.loading = true;
                    this.error = null;
                    fetch(atob('aHR0cHM6Ly9maWxtaXpsZWhlbGwubmV0L2FqYXgvY29tbWVudC1zdG9yZQ=='), {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: {
                            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken,
                            'X-XSRF-TOKEN': xsrfToken,
                            'Accept': 'application/json'
                        },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            movie_id: 3730,
                            movie_slug: "orumcek-adam-yepyeni-bir-gun-jg6",
                            comment: this.comment,
                            name: this.name,
                            email: this.email,
                            has_spoiler: this.hasSpoiler,
                            parent_id: parentId,
                            'cf-turnstile-response': tokenInput.value,
                            'g-recaptcha-response': tokenInput.value
                        })
                    }).then(function(res) {
                        return res.json().then(function(data) {
                            return { status: res.status, body: data };
                        });
                    }).then(function(res) {
                        self.loading = false;
                        if (res.status === 200 || res.status === 201) {
                            self.success = res.body.message;
                            self.quotedReply = null;
                            setTimeout(function() { window.location.reload(); }, 1500);
                        } else {
                            var errorMsg = res.body.message;
                            if (res.body.errors) {
                                errorMsg = Object.values(res.body.errors).flat()[0];
                            }
                            self.error = errorMsg + (res.body.reason ? ' (' + res.body.reason + ')' : '');
                            if (window.turnstile) { try { window.turnstile.reset('#comment-turnstile'); } catch(e) {} }
                            if (window.grecaptcha) { try { window.grecaptcha.reset(); } catch(e) {} }
                        }
                    }).catch(function() {
                        self.loading = false;
                        self.error = 'Bir hata oluştu. Lütfen tekrar deneyin.';
                        if (window.turnstile) { try { window.turnstile.reset('#comment-turnstile'); } catch(e) {} }
                        if (window.grecaptcha) { try { window.grecaptcha.reset(); } catch(e) {} }
                    });
                }
            };
        };
    </script>

    <!-- Report Modal -->
    <div x-show="isReportModalOpen"

         class="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4"

             x-data="setupReportModal()"
             data-csrf="9cCFG37dw9I0qxNfFMoh7chWVAw6HXZM0YlQ6Pji">

                </button>
            </div>

            <!-- Messages -->
            <div x-show="reportError" class="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 text-red-400 p-3 rounded text-sm font-bold" x-text="reportError" style="display:none"></div>
            <div x-show="reportSuccess && (!telegramBotUrl || telegramLinked || !wantTelegram)" class="bg-green-500/10 border border-green-500/20 text-green-400 p-3 rounded text-sm font-bold" x-text="reportSuccess" style="display:none"></div>

            <!-- Telegram Deep Link Kartı (Botu Başlat) -->
            <div x-show="reportSuccess && telegramBotUrl && wantTelegram && !telegramLinked" class="p-4 bg-sky-500/10 border border-sky-500/30 rounded-xl space-y-3 text-center my-3" style="display:none">
                <div class="text-sm font-bold text-sky-300 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="m22 2-7 20-4-9-9-4Z"/><path d="M22 2 11 13"/></svg>
                    Talebiniz Alındı! Botu Başlatın
                </div>
                <p class="text-xs text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
                    Hata çözüldüğünde Telegram üzerinden özel bildirim alabilmek için botumuzda <b>"Başlat"</b> butonuna basmanız gerekmektedir:
                </p>
                <div class="pt-1 flex flex-col gap-2">
                    <a :href="telegramBotUrl" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[#229ED9] hover:bg-[#1E88E5] text-white font-black text-xs px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-all shadow-lg shadow-sky-500/20 w-full uppercase tracking-wider">
                        <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="m22 2-7 20-4-9-9-4Z"/><path d="M22 2 11 13"/></svg>
                        Telegram Botunu Aç ve Başlat
                    </a>
                    <button type="button" @click="$dispatch('close-report-modal'); resetState()" class="text-[11px] text-gray-400 hover:text-white underline py-1">
                        Tamamlandı / Kapat
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <form x-show="!(reportSuccess && telegramBotUrl && wantTelegram && !telegramLinked)" class="space-y-4" @submit.prevent="submitReport" x-ref="reportForm">
                                <div class="space-y-2">
                    <label class="text-[10px] font-black text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">HATA TÜRÜ</label>
                    <select x-model="reportType" class="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-accent transition-colors text-sm appearance-none">
                        <option value="" disabled selected>Seçiniz...</option>
                        <option value="video_broken">Video Açılmıyor / Donuyor</option>
                        <option value="sound_sync">Ses Kayması Var</option>
                        <option value="subtitle">Altyazı Hatalı / Eksik</option>
                        <option value="other">Diğer</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="space-y-2">
                    <label class="text-[10px] font-black text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-widest">AÇIKLAMA</label>
                    <textarea x-model="reportDescription" rows="3" placeholder="Detay verirseniz daha hızlı çözeriz..." class="w-full bg-black/40 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-white placeholder-white/30 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent transition-colors text-sm resize-none"></textarea>
                </div>

                <!-- Telegram Bildirim Opsiyonu -->
                <div class="p-3 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-lg space-y-2">

         style="display: none;"
         x-transition>
        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm" @click="isCommentModalOpen = false"></div>
        <div class="bg-[#1a1a1a] w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-white/10 p-6 relative shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)] z-[101]"
             x-data="setupCommentModal()"
             data-csrf="9cCFG37dw9I0qxNfFMoh7chWVAw6HXZM0YlQ6Pji"
             @quote-comment.window="quotedReply = $event.detail"
             @open-comment-modal.window="quotedReply = null">
            <!-- Header -->
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                <h2 class="text-xl font-black italic tracking-tighter uppercase text-white">YORUM YAP</h2>
                <button @click="isCommentModalOpen = false" class="text-muted-foreground hover:text-white transition-colors">
                    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M18 6 6 18"/><path d="m6 6 12 12"/></svg>
                </button>
            </div>
            
            <!-- Success Message -->
            <div x-show="success" class="mb-4 bg-green-500/10 border border-green-500/20 text-green-400 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2" style="display:none">
                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M22 11.08V12a10 10 0 1 1-5.93-9.14"/><polyline points="22 4 12 14.01 9 11.01"/></svg>
                <span x-text="success"></span>
            </div>
            
            <!-- Error Message -->
            <div x-show="error" class="mb-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 text-red-400 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold" x-text="error" style="display:none"></div>

            <!-- Quoted Comment Preview -->
            <div x-show="quotedReply" class="mb-6 p-4 bg-white/5 rounded-xl border-l-4 border-accent relative" style="display:none">

    <style>
    @keyframes commentHighlightPulse {
        0%, 100% {
            border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(243, 156, 18, 0);
        }
        50% {
            border-color: rgba(243, 156, 18, 0.75);
            box-shadow: 0 0 25px 2px rgba(243, 156, 18, 0.35);
        }
    }
    .comment-highlight {
        animation: commentHighlightPulse 1.2s ease-in-out 3 !important;
    }
</style>
<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        try {
            const hash = window.location.hash;
            if (hash && hash.startsWith('#comment-')) {
                const targetId = hash.substring(1);
                
                const scrollToTarget = () => {
                    const targetEl = document.getElementById(targetId);
                    if (!targetEl) return false;
                    
                    // Reveal hidden comments (trigger Alpine state + remove Tailwind hidden class)
                    const hiddenParent = targetEl.closest('.hidden-comment');
                    if (hiddenParent || targetEl.offsetParent === null) {
                        const loadMoreBtn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button')).find(btn => btn.textContent && btn.textContent.includes('Daha Fazla Yorum'));
                        if (loadMoreBtn) {
                            try { loadMoreBtn.click(); } catch(e) {}
                        }
                        document.querySelectorAll('.hidden-comment').forEach(el => {
                            el.classList.remove('hidden');
                            el.style.display = 'block';
                        });
                    }
                    
                    targetEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
                    targetEl.classList.add('comment-highlight');
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        targetEl.classList.remove('comment-highlight');
                    }, 4000);

                    return true;
                };

                const execute = () => {
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const ok = scrollToTarget();
                        if (ok) {
                            setTimeout(() => {
                                const el = document.getElementById(targetId);
                                if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
                            }, 500);
                        }
                    }, 300);
                };

                if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
                    execute();
                } else {
                    window.addEventListener('load', execute);
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Comment scroll error:', e);
        }
    });

    // İzlenme Sayacı (LocalStorage Korumalı + AJAX)
    window.recordMovieView = function() {
        if (window._movieViewRecorded) return;
        window._movieViewRecorded = true;

        try {
            const movieId = 3730;
            const storageKey = 'movie_view_' + movieId;
            const now = Date.now();
            const EXPIRE_TIME = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24 saat

            // 1. LocalStorage Kontrolü (Son 24 saatte izlendiyse istek atma - Sunucuya 0 yük)
            const lastViewed = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
            if (lastViewed && (now - parseInt(lastViewed, 10)) < EXPIRE_TIME) {
                return;
            }

            // 2. LocalStorage'a kaydet
            localStorage.setItem(storageKey, now.toString());

            // 3. Otomatik Temizlik: 24 saati geçmiş eski izlenme kayıtlarını sil
            try {
                for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
                    const key = localStorage.key(i);
                    if (key && key.startsWith('movie_view_')) {
                        const val = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(key), 10);
                        if (val && (now - val) > EXPIRE_TIME) {
                            localStorage.removeItem(key);
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (e) {}

            // 4. Asenkron AJAX İsteği (Relative URL - CORS Sorunsuz)
            const url = '/film/3730/izlendi';
            fetch(url, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    'Accept': 'application/json'
                }
            })
            .then(function(res) {
                if (res.ok) return res.json();
                return null;
            })
            .then(function(data) {
                if (data && data.views_count) {
                    var el = document.getElementById('movie-views-count');
                    if (el) {
                        el.textContent = data.views_count;
                    }
                }
            })
            .catch(function(err) {
                console.warn('View record error:', err);
            });
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('LocalStorage error:', e);
        }
    };
</script>
    <script>
        /* Scroll Effect for Navbar */
        window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
            var header = document.getElementById('site-header');
            if (window.scrollY > 20) {
                header.classList.add('py-1', 'shadow-lg');
                header.classList.remove('py-2');
            } else {
                header.classList.remove('py-1', 'shadow-lg');
                header.classList.add('py-2');
            }
        });
    </script>

                        </svg>

                    <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-3" id="modal-title" x-text="mode === 'new' ? 'FilmizleHell VIP Bildirimleri' : 'Bildirimler Açık'"></h3>
                    
                    <p class="text-sm leading-relaxed text-gray-400 mb-8 px-2" x-text="mode === 'new' ? 'Sinemaya yeni düşen filmleri ve en iyi başyapıtları herkesten önce izleme şansını kaçırma! Harika bir film eklendiğinde anında haberin olsun, ilk izleyen sen ol!' : 'Bildirim sistemimize zaten kayıtlısınız. VIP üyemiz olarak yeni filmler eklendiğinde ilk sizin haberiniz olacak.'"></p>

                    <template x-if="mode === 'new'">
                        <button @click="subscribeToPush().finally(() => { show = false; })" type="button" class="w-full rounded-xl bg-[#ffc107] px-4 py-3.5 text-base font-bold text-black shadow-lg hover:bg-[#ffca28] active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-200">
                            Evet, Bildirimleri Aç
                        </button>
                    </template>
                    <template x-if="mode === 'already'">
                        <div class="w-full text-center p-3 bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10 mb-3">
                            <span class="text-sm text-gray-400">Bildirimleri tarayıcınızın sol üst köşesindeki kilit (<svg class="inline w-3 h-3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 15v2m-6 4h12a2 2 0 002-2v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2zm10-10V7a4 4 0 00-8 0v4h8z"></path></svg>) ikonuna tıklayarak yönetebilirsiniz.</span>
                        </div>
                    </template>
                    <template x-if="mode === 'already'">
                        <button @click="show = false" type="button" class="mt-3 w-full rounded-xl bg-[#ffc107] px-4 py-3.5 text-base font-bold text-black shadow-lg hover:bg-[#ffca28] active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-200">
                            Tamam, Kapat
                        </button>
                    </template>

                    <template x-if="mode === 'new'">
                        <button @click="show = false" type="button" class="mt-5 text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 hover:text-white underline decoration-gray-500 underline-offset-4 transition-colors">Daha Sonra</button>
                    </template>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>



    <!-- Search Placeholder Typewriter Rotator -->
    <script>
        (function() {
            const phrases = [
                "Film adı ile ara... (örn: Matrix)",
                "IMDb ID ile ara... (örn: tt0111161)",
                "IMDb linki ile ara..."
            ];

            const defaultPlaceholder = "Film ara...";
            let phraseIndex = 0;
            let charIndex = 0;
            let isDeleting = false;

            function getActiveInputs() {
                return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[data-rotating-placeholder="true"]')).filter(function(input) {
                    return document.activeElement !== input && (!input.value || input.value.trim() === '');
                });
            }

            function tick() {
                const currentPhrase = phrases[phraseIndex];
                let currentText = '';

                if (isDeleting) {
                    currentText = currentPhrase.substring(0, charIndex - 1);
                    charIndex--;
                } else {
                    currentText = currentPhrase.substring(0, charIndex + 1);
                    charIndex++;
                }

                const targets = getActiveInputs();
                const displayPlaceholder = currentText.length > 0 ? currentText : defaultPlaceholder;
                for (let i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) {
                    targets[i].setAttribute('placeholder', displayPlaceholder);
                }

                let speed = isDeleting ? 30 : 60;

                if (!isDeleting && charIndex === currentPhrase.length) {
                    isDeleting = true;
                    speed = 2200;
                } else if (isDeleting && charIndex === 0) {
                    isDeleting = false;
                    phraseIndex = (phraseIndex + 1) % phrases.length;
                    speed = 400;
                }

                setTimeout(tick, speed);
            }

            function setupListeners() {
                const allInputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[data-rotating-placeholder="true"]');
                allInputs.forEach(function(input) {
                    input.addEventListener('focus', function() {
                        this.setAttribute('placeholder', defaultPlaceholder);
                    });
                    input.addEventListener('blur', function() {
                        if (!this.value || this.value.trim() === '') {
                            const cur = phrases[phraseIndex].substring(0, charIndex);
                            this.setAttribute('placeholder', cur.length > 0 ? cur : defaultPlaceholder);
                        }
                    });
                });
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
                    setupListeners();
                    setTimeout(tick, 1000);
                });
            } else {
                setupListeners();
                setTimeout(tick, 1000);
            }
        })();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
